  <section id="b508-snmp-insecure-version">
<h1>B508: snmp_insecure_version<a class="headerlink" href="#b508-snmp-insecure-version" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.snmp_security_check.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">snmp_insecure_version_check</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/snmp_security_check.html#snmp_insecure_version_check"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B508: Checking for insecure SNMP versions</strong></p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>This test is for checking for the usage of insecure SNMP version like</dt><dd><p>v1, v2c</p>
</dd>
</dl>
<p>Please update your code to use more secure versions of SNMP.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B508:snmp_insecure_version_check] The use of SNMPv1 and
   SNMPv2 is insecure. You should use SNMPv3 if able.
   Severity: Medium Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-319 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html)
   Location: examples/snmp.py:4:4
   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/latest/plugins/b508_snmp_insecure_version_check.html
3   # SHOULD FAIL
4   a = CommunityData(&#39;public&#39;, mpModel=0)
5   # SHOULD FAIL
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="http://snmplabs.com/pysnmp/examples/hlapi/asyncore/sync/manager/cmdgen/snmp-versions.html">http://snmplabs.com/pysnmp/examples/hlapi/asyncore/sync/manager/cmdgen/snmp-versions.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 1.7.2.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>
</dd></dl>

</section>

  <section id="b509-snmp-weak-cryptography">
<h1>B509: snmp_weak_cryptography<a class="headerlink" href="#b509-snmp-weak-cryptography" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.snmp_security_check.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">snmp_crypto_check</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/snmp_security_check.html#snmp_crypto_check"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B509: Checking for weak cryptography</strong></p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>This test is for checking for the usage of insecure SNMP cryptography:</dt><dd><p>v3 using noAuthNoPriv.</p>
</dd>
</dl>
<p>Please update your code to use more secure versions of SNMP. For example:</p>
<dl class="simple">
<dt>Instead of:</dt><dd><p><cite>CommunityData(‘public’, mpModel=0)</cite></p>
</dd>
<dt>Use (Defaults to usmHMACMD5AuthProtocol and usmDESPrivProtocol</dt><dd><p><cite>UsmUserData(“securityName”, “authName”, “privName”)</cite></p>
</dd>
</dl>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B509:snmp_crypto_check] You should not use SNMPv3 without encryption. noAuthNoPriv &amp; authNoPriv is insecure
   Severity: Medium CWE: CWE-319 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html) Confidence: High
   Location: examples/snmp.py:6:11
   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/latest/plugins/b509_snmp_crypto_check.html
5   # SHOULD FAIL
6   insecure = UsmUserData(&quot;securityName&quot;)
7   # SHOULD FAIL
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="http://snmplabs.com/pysnmp/examples/hlapi/asyncore/sync/manager/cmdgen/snmp-versions.html">http://snmplabs.com/pysnmp/examples/hlapi/asyncore/sync/manager/cmdgen/snmp-versions.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/319.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 1.7.2.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>
</dd></dl>

</section>

  <section id="b601-paramiko-calls">
<h1>B601: paramiko_calls<a class="headerlink" href="#b601-paramiko-calls" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b601-test-for-shell-injection-within-paramiko">
<h2>B601: Test for shell injection within Paramiko<a class="headerlink" href="#b601-test-for-shell-injection-within-paramiko" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>Paramiko is a Python library designed to work with the SSH2 protocol for secure
(encrypted and authenticated) connections to remote machines. It is intended to
run commands on a remote host. These commands are run within a shell on the
target and are thus vulnerable to various shell injection attacks. Bandit
reports a MEDIUM issue when it detects the use of Paramiko’s “exec_command”
method advising the user to check inputs are correctly sanitized.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Possible shell injection via Paramiko call, check inputs are
   properly sanitized.
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: Medium
   CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/paramiko_injection.py:4
3    # this is not safe
4    paramiko.exec_command(&#39;something; really; unsafe&#39;)
5
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org">https://security.openstack.org</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/paramiko/paramiko">https://github.com/paramiko/paramiko</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://www.owasp.org/index.php/Command_Injection">https://www.owasp.org/index.php/Command_Injection</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 0.12.0.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>

  <section id="b602-subprocess-popen-with-shell-equals-true">
<h1>B602: subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true<a class="headerlink" href="#b602-subprocess-popen-with-shell-equals-true" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.injection_shell.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em>, <em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">config</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/injection_shell.html#subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B602: Test for use of popen with shell equals true</strong></p>
<p>Python possesses many mechanisms to invoke an external executable. However,
doing so may present a security issue if appropriate care is not taken to
sanitize any user provided or variable input.</p>
<p>This plugin test is part of a family of tests built to check for process
spawning and warn appropriately. Specifically, this test looks for the
spawning of a subprocess using a command shell. This type of subprocess
invocation is dangerous as it is vulnerable to various shell injection
attacks. Great care should be taken to sanitize all input in order to
mitigate this risk. Calls of this type are identified by a parameter of
‘shell=True’ being given.</p>
<p>Additionally, this plugin scans the command string given and adjusts its
reported severity based on how it is presented. If the command string is a
simple static string containing no special shell characters, then the
resulting issue has low severity. If the string is static, but contains
shell formatting characters or wildcards, then the reported issue is
medium. Finally, if the string is computed using Python’s string
manipulation or formatting operations, then the reported issue has high
severity. These severity levels reflect the likelihood that the code is
vulnerable to injection.</p>
<p>See also:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/linux_commands_wildcard_injection</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_without_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_no_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_a_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_partial_path</span></p></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin test shares a configuration with others in the same family,
namely <cite>shell_injection</cite>. This configuration is divided up into three
sections, <cite>subprocess</cite>, <cite>shell</cite> and <cite>no_shell</cite>. They each list Python calls
that spawn subprocesses, invoke commands within a shell, or invoke commands
without a shell (by replacing the calling process) respectively.</p>
<p>This plugin specifically scans for methods listed in <cite>subprocess</cite> section
that have shell=True specified.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">shell_injection</span><span class="p">:</span>

<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process using the subprocess module, or one of its</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">wrappers.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess</span><span class="p p-Indicator">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.Popen</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.call</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: subprocess call with shell=True seems safe, but may be
changed in the future, consider rewriting without shell
   Severity: Low   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/subprocess_shell.py:21
20  subprocess.check_call([&#39;/bin/ls&#39;, &#39;-l&#39;], shell=False)
21  subprocess.check_call(&#39;/bin/ls -l&#39;, shell=True)
22

&gt;&gt; Issue: call with shell=True contains special shell characters,
consider moving extra logic into Python code
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/subprocess_shell.py:26
25
26  subprocess.Popen(&#39;/bin/ls *&#39;, shell=True)
27  subprocess.Popen(&#39;/bin/ls %s&#39; % (&#39;something&#39;,), shell=True)

&gt;&gt; Issue: subprocess call with shell=True identified, security issue.
   Severity: High   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/subprocess_shell.py:27
26  subprocess.Popen(&#39;/bin/ls *&#39;, shell=True)
27  subprocess.Popen(&#39;/bin/ls %s&#39; % (&#39;something&#39;,), shell=True)
28  subprocess.Popen(&#39;/bin/ls {}&#39;.format(&#39;something&#39;), shell=True)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org">https://security.openstack.org</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/subprocess.html#frequently-used-arguments">https://docs.python.org/3/library/subprocess.html#frequently-used-arguments</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_avoid-shell-true.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_avoid-shell-true.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>

</div>

</div>
</dd></dl>

          <div role="main" class="document" itemscope="itemscope" itemtype="http://schema.org/Article">
           <div itemprop="articleBody">
             
  <section id="b603-subprocess-without-shell-equals-true">
<h1>B603: subprocess_without_shell_equals_true<a class="headerlink" href="#b603-subprocess-without-shell-equals-true" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.injection_shell.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">subprocess_without_shell_equals_true</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em>, <em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">config</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/injection_shell.html#subprocess_without_shell_equals_true"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B603: Test for use of subprocess without shell equals true</strong></p>
<p>Python possesses many mechanisms to invoke an external executable. However,
doing so may present a security issue if appropriate care is not taken to
sanitize any user provided or variable input.</p>
<p>This plugin test is part of a family of tests built to check for process
spawning and warn appropriately. Specifically, this test looks for the
spawning of a subprocess without the use of a command shell. This type of
subprocess invocation is not vulnerable to shell injection attacks, but
care should still be taken to ensure validity of input.</p>
<p>Because this is a lesser issue than that described in
<cite>subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true</cite> a LOW severity warning is
reported.</p>
<p>See also:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/linux_commands_wildcard_injection</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_no_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_a_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_partial_path</span></p></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin test shares a configuration with others in the same family,
namely <cite>shell_injection</cite>. This configuration is divided up into three
sections, <cite>subprocess</cite>, <cite>shell</cite> and <cite>no_shell</cite>. They each list Python calls
that spawn subprocesses, invoke commands within a shell, or invoke commands
without a shell (by replacing the calling process) respectively.</p>
<p>This plugin specifically scans for methods listed in <cite>subprocess</cite> section
that have shell=False specified.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">shell_injection</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process using the subprocess module, or one of its</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">wrappers.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess</span><span class="p p-Indicator">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.Popen</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.call</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: subprocess call - check for execution of untrusted input.
   Severity: Low   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/subprocess_shell.py:23
22
23    subprocess.check_output([&#39;/bin/ls&#39;, &#39;-l&#39;])
24
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org">https://security.openstack.org</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/subprocess.html#frequently-used-arguments">https://docs.python.org/3/library/subprocess.html#frequently-used-arguments</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_avoid-shell-true.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_avoid-shell-true.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 0.9.0.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>
</dd></dl>

           <div itemprop="articleBody">
             
  <section id="b604-any-other-function-with-shell-equals-true">
<h1>B604: any_other_function_with_shell_equals_true<a class="headerlink" href="#b604-any-other-function-with-shell-equals-true" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.injection_shell.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">any_other_function_with_shell_equals_true</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em>, <em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">config</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/injection_shell.html#any_other_function_with_shell_equals_true"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B604: Test for any function with shell equals true</strong></p>
<p>Python possesses many mechanisms to invoke an external executable. However,
doing so may present a security issue if appropriate care is not taken to
sanitize any user provided or variable input.</p>
<p>This plugin test is part of a family of tests built to check for process
spawning and warn appropriately. Specifically, this plugin test
interrogates method calls for the presence of a keyword parameter <cite>shell</cite>
equalling true. It is related to detection of shell injection issues and is
intended to catch custom wrappers to vulnerable methods that may have been
created.</p>
<p>See also:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/linux_commands_wildcard_injection</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_without_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_no_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_a_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_partial_path</span></p></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin test shares a configuration with others in the same family,
namely <cite>shell_injection</cite>. This configuration is divided up into three
sections, <cite>subprocess</cite>, <cite>shell</cite> and <cite>no_shell</cite>. They each list Python calls
that spawn subprocesses, invoke commands within a shell, or invoke commands
without a shell (by replacing the calling process) respectively.</p>
<p>Specifically, this plugin excludes those functions listed under the
subprocess section, these methods are tested in a separate specific test
plugin and this exclusion prevents duplicate issue reporting.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">shell_injection</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process using the subprocess module, or one of its</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">wrappers.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess</span><span class="p p-Indicator">:</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="p p-Indicator">[</span><span class="nv">subprocess.Popen</span><span class="p p-Indicator">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nv">subprocess.call</span><span class="p p-Indicator">,</span>
<span class="w">                 </span><span class="nv">subprocess.check_call</span><span class="p p-Indicator">,</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="nv">subprocess.check_output</span>
<span class="w">                 </span><span class="nv">execute_with_timeout</span><span class="p p-Indicator">]</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Function call with shell=True parameter identified, possible
security issue.
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/subprocess_shell.py:9
8 pop(&#39;/bin/gcc --version&#39;, shell=True)
9 Popen(&#39;/bin/gcc --version&#39;, shell=True)
10
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_avoid-shell-true.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_avoid-shell-true.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 0.9.0.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>
</dd></dl>

          <div role="main" class="document" itemscope="itemscope" itemtype="http://schema.org/Article">
           <div itemprop="articleBody">
             
  <section id="b605-start-process-with-a-shell">
<h1>B605: start_process_with_a_shell<a class="headerlink" href="#b605-start-process-with-a-shell" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.injection_shell.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">start_process_with_a_shell</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em>, <em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">config</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/injection_shell.html#start_process_with_a_shell"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B605: Test for starting a process with a shell</strong></p>
<p>Python possesses many mechanisms to invoke an external executable. However,
doing so may present a security issue if appropriate care is not taken to
sanitize any user provided or variable input.</p>
<p>This plugin test is part of a family of tests built to check for process
spawning and warn appropriately. Specifically, this test looks for the
spawning of a subprocess using a command shell. This type of subprocess
invocation is dangerous as it is vulnerable to various shell injection
attacks. Great care should be taken to sanitize all input in order to
mitigate this risk. Calls of this type are identified by the use of certain
commands which are known to use shells. Bandit will report a LOW
severity warning.</p>
<p>See also:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/linux_commands_wildcard_injection</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_without_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_no_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_partial_path</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin test shares a configuration with others in the same family,
namely <cite>shell_injection</cite>. This configuration is divided up into three
sections, <cite>subprocess</cite>, <cite>shell</cite> and <cite>no_shell</cite>. They each list Python calls
that spawn subprocesses, invoke commands within a shell, or invoke commands
without a shell (by replacing the calling process) respectively.</p>
<p>This plugin specifically scans for methods listed in <cite>shell</cite> section.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">shell_injection</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">shell</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.system</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.popen</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.popen2</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.popen3</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.popen4</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.popen2</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.popen3</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.popen4</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.Popen3</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.Popen4</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">commands.getoutput</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">commands.getstatusoutput</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.getoutput</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.getstatusoutput</span>
</pre></div>
</div>

<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>

          <div role="main" class="document" itemscope="itemscope" itemtype="http://schema.org/Article">
           <div itemprop="articleBody">
             
  <section id="b606-start-process-with-no-shell">
<h1>B606: start_process_with_no_shell<a class="headerlink" href="#b606-start-process-with-no-shell" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.injection_shell.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">start_process_with_no_shell</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em>, <em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">config</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/injection_shell.html#start_process_with_no_shell"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B606: Test for starting a process with no shell</strong></p>
<p>Python possesses many mechanisms to invoke an external executable. However,
doing so may present a security issue if appropriate care is not taken to
sanitize any user provided or variable input.</p>
<p>This plugin test is part of a family of tests built to check for process
spawning and warn appropriately. Specifically, this test looks for the
spawning of a subprocess in a way that doesn’t use a shell. Although this
is generally safe, it maybe useful for penetration testing workflows to
track where external system calls are used.  As such a LOW severity message
is generated.</p>
<p>See also:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/linux_commands_wildcard_injection</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_without_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_a_shell</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/start_process_with_partial_path</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="xref std std-doc">../plugins/subprocess_popen_with_shell_equals_true</span></p></li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin test shares a configuration with others in the same family,
namely <cite>shell_injection</cite>. This configuration is divided up into three
sections, <cite>subprocess</cite>, <cite>shell</cite> and <cite>no_shell</cite>. They each list Python calls
that spawn subprocesses, invoke commands within a shell, or invoke commands
without a shell (by replacing the calling process) respectively.</p>
<p>This plugin specifically scans for methods listed in <cite>no_shell</cite> section.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">shell_injection</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">no_shell</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execl</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execle</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execlp</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execlpe</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execv</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execve</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execvp</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execvpe</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnl</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnle</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnlp</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnlpe</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnv</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnve</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnvp</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.spawnvpe</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.startfile</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [start_process_with_no_shell] Starting a process without a
   shell.
   Severity: Low   Confidence: Medium
   CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: examples/os-spawn.py:8
7   os.spawnv(mode, path, args)
8   os.spawnve(mode, path, args, env)
9   os.spawnvp(mode, file, args)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org">https://security.openstack.org</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/os.html#os.system">https://docs.python.org/3/library/os.html#os.system</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/subprocess.html#frequently-used-arguments">https://docs.python.org/3/library/subprocess.html#frequently-used-arguments</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_use-subprocess-securely.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="versionchanged">

</div>
</dd></dl>

          <div role="main" class="document" itemscope="itemscope" itemtype="http://schema.org/Article">
           <div itemprop="articleBody">
             
  <section id="b607-start-process-with-partial-path">
<h1>B607: start_process_with_partial_path<a class="headerlink" href="#b607-start-process-with-partial-path" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.injection_shell.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">start_process_with_partial_path</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em>, <em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">config</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/injection_shell.html#start_process_with_partial_path"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B607: Test for starting a process with a partial path</strong></p>
<p>Python possesses many mechanisms to invoke an external executable. If the
desired executable path is not fully qualified relative to the filesystem
root then this may present a potential security risk.</p>
<p>In POSIX environments, the <cite>PATH</cite> environment variable is used to specify a
set of standard locations that will be searched for the first matching
named executable. While convenient, this behavior may allow a malicious
actor to exert control over a system. If they are able to adjust the
contents of the <cite>PATH</cite> variable, or manipulate the file system, then a
bogus executable may be discovered in place of the desired one. This
executable will be invoked with the user privileges of the Python process
that spawned it, potentially a highly privileged user.</p>
<p>This test will scan the parameters of all configured Python methods,
looking for paths that do not start at the filesystem root, that is, do not
have a leading ‘/’ character.</p>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin test shares a configuration with others in the same family,
namely <cite>shell_injection</cite>. This configuration is divided up into three
sections, <cite>subprocess</cite>, <cite>shell</cite> and <cite>no_shell</cite>. They each list Python calls
that spawn subprocesses, invoke commands within a shell, or invoke commands
without a shell (by replacing the calling process) respectively.</p>
<p>This test will scan parameters of all methods in all sections. Note that
methods are fully qualified and de-aliased prior to checking.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">shell_injection</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process using the subprocess module, or one of its</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">wrappers.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess</span><span class="p p-Indicator">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.Popen</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.call</span>

<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process with a function vulnerable to shell injection.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">shell</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.system</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.popen</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.Popen3</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.Popen4</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">commands.getoutput</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">commands.getstatusoutput</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process with a function that is not vulnerable to shell</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">injection.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">no_shell</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execl</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execle</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Starting a process with a partial executable path
Severity: Low   Confidence: High
CWE: CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
Location: ./examples/partial_path_process.py:3
2    from subprocess import Popen as pop
3    pop(&#39;gcc --version&#39;, shell=False)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org">https://security.openstack.org</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/os.html#process-management">https://docs.python.org/3/library/os.html#process-management</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">

<div class="versionchanged">

</div>
</dd></dl>

  <section id="b608-hardcoded-sql-expressions">
<h1>B608: hardcoded_sql_expressions<a class="headerlink" href="#b608-hardcoded-sql-expressions" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b608-test-for-sql-injection">
<h2>B608: Test for SQL injection<a class="headerlink" href="#b608-test-for-sql-injection" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>An SQL injection attack consists of insertion or “injection” of a SQL query via
the input data given to an application. It is a very common attack vector. This
plugin test looks for strings that resemble SQL statements that are involved in
some form of string building operation. For example:</p>
<blockquote>
<div><ul class="simple">
<li><p>“SELECT %s FROM derp;” % var</p></li>
<li><p>“SELECT thing FROM “ + tab</p></li>
<li><p>“SELECT “ + val + “ FROM “ + tab + …</p></li>
<li><p>“SELECT {} FROM derp;”.format(var)</p></li>
<li><p>f”SELECT foo FROM bar WHERE id = {product}”</p></li>
</ul>
</div></blockquote>
<p>Unless care is taken to sanitize and control the input data when building such
SQL statement strings, an injection attack becomes possible. If strings of this
nature are discovered, a LOW confidence issue is reported. In order to boost
result confidence, this plugin test will also check to see if the discovered
string is in use with standard Python DBAPI calls <cite>execute</cite> or <cite>executemany</cite>.
If so, a MEDIUM issue is reported. For example:</p>
<blockquote>
<div><ul class="simple">
<li><p>cursor.execute(“SELECT %s FROM derp;” % var)</p></li>
</ul>
</div></blockquote>
<p>Use of str.replace in the string construction can also be dangerous.
For example:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>“SELECT * FROM foo WHERE id = ‘[VALUE]’”.replace(“[VALUE]”, identifier)</p></li>
</ul>
<p>However, such cases are always reported with LOW confidence to compensate
for false positives, since valid uses of str.replace can be common.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Possible SQL injection vector through string-based query
construction.
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: Low
   CWE: CWE-89 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html)
   Location: ./examples/sql_statements.py:4
3 query = &quot;DELETE FROM foo WHERE id = &#39;%s&#39;&quot; % identifier
4 query = &quot;UPDATE foo SET value = &#39;b&#39; WHERE id = &#39;%s&#39;&quot; % identifier
5
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://www.owasp.org/index.php/SQL_Injection">https://www.owasp.org/index.php/SQL_Injection</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_parameterize-database-queries.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_parameterize-database-queries.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 0.9.0.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.7: </span>Flag when str.replace is used in the string construction</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>

  <section id="b609-linux-commands-wildcard-injection">
<h1>B609: linux_commands_wildcard_injection<a class="headerlink" href="#b609-linux-commands-wildcard-injection" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b609-test-for-use-of-wildcard-injection">
<h2>B609: Test for use of wildcard injection<a class="headerlink" href="#b609-test-for-use-of-wildcard-injection" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>Python provides a number of methods that emulate the behavior of standard Linux
command line utilities. Like their Linux counterparts, these commands may take
a wildcard “*” character in place of a file system path. This is interpreted
to mean “any and all files or folders” and can be used to build partially
qualified paths, such as “/home/user/*”.</p>
<p>The use of partially qualified paths may result in unintended consequences if
an unexpected file or symlink is placed into the path location given. This
becomes particularly dangerous when combined with commands used to manipulate
file permissions or copy data off of a system.</p>
<p>This test plugin looks for usage of the following commands in conjunction with
wild card parameters:</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p>‘chown’</p></li>
<li><p>‘chmod’</p></li>
<li><p>‘tar’</p></li>
<li><p>‘rsync’</p></li>
</ul>
<p>As well as any method configured in the shell or subprocess injection test
configurations.</p>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin test shares a configuration with others in the same family, namely
<cite>shell_injection</cite>. This configuration is divided up into three sections,
<cite>subprocess</cite>, <cite>shell</cite> and <cite>no_shell</cite>. They each list Python calls that spawn
subprocesses, invoke commands within a shell, or invoke commands without a
shell (by replacing the calling process) respectively.</p>
<p>This test will scan parameters of all methods in all sections. Note that
methods are fully qualified and de-aliased prior to checking.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">shell_injection</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process using the subprocess module, or one of its wrappers.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">subprocess</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.Popen</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">subprocess.call</span>

<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process with a function vulnerable to shell injection.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">shell</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.system</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.popen</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.Popen3</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">popen2.Popen4</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">commands.getoutput</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">commands.getstatusoutput</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Start a process with a function that is not vulnerable to shell</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">injection.</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">no_shell</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execl</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">os.execle</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Possible wildcard injection in call: subprocess.Popen
   Severity: High   Confidence: Medium
   CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/wildcard-injection.py:8
7    o.popen2(&#39;/bin/chmod *&#39;)
8    subp.Popen(&#39;/bin/chown *&#39;, shell=True)
9

&gt;&gt; Issue: subprocess call - check for execution of untrusted input.
   Severity: Low   Confidence: High
   CWE-78 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html)
   Location: ./examples/wildcard-injection.py:11
10   # Not vulnerable to wildcard injection
11   subp.Popen(&#39;/bin/rsync *&#39;)
12   subp.Popen(&quot;/bin/chmod *&quot;)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org">https://security.openstack.org</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wildcard_character">https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wildcard_character</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://www.defensecode.com/public/DefenseCode_Unix_WildCards_Gone_Wild.txt">https://www.defensecode.com/public/DefenseCode_Unix_WildCards_Gone_Wild.txt</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/78.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>

  <section id="b610-django-extra-used">
<h1>B610: django_extra_used<a class="headerlink" href="#b610-django-extra-used" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.django_sql_injection.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">django_extra_used</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/django_sql_injection.html#django_extra_used"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B610: Potential SQL injection on extra function</strong></p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B610:django_extra_used] Use of extra potential SQL attack vector.
   Severity: Medium Confidence: Medium
   CWE: CWE-89 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html)
   Location: examples/django_sql_injection_extra.py:29:0
   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/latest/plugins/b610_django_extra_used.html
28  tables_str = &#39;django_content_type&quot; WHERE &quot;auth_user&quot;.&quot;username&quot;=&quot;admin&#39;
29  User.objects.all().extra(tables=[tables_str]).distinct()
</pre></div>
</div>

<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/dev/topics/security/#sql-injection-protection">https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/dev/topics/security/#sql-injection-protection</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html</a></p></li>
</ul>

  <section id="b611-django-rawsql-used">
<h1>B611: django_rawsql_used<a class="headerlink" href="#b611-django-rawsql-used" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.django_sql_injection.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">django_rawsql_used</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/django_sql_injection.html#django_rawsql_used"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B611: Potential SQL injection on RawSQL function</strong></p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B611:django_rawsql_used] Use of RawSQL potential SQL attack vector.
   Severity: Medium Confidence: Medium
   CWE: CWE-89 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html)
   Location: examples/django_sql_injection_raw.py:11:26
   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/latest/plugins/b611_django_rawsql_used.html
10        &#39; WHERE &quot;username&quot;=&quot;admin&quot; OR 1=%s --&#39;
11  User.objects.annotate(val=RawSQL(raw, [0]))
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/dev/topics/security/#sql-injection-protection">https://docs.djangoproject.com/en/dev/topics/security/#sql-injection-protection</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 1.5.0.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>
</dd></dl>

  <section id="b612-logging-config-insecure-listen">
<h1>B612: logging_config_insecure_listen<a class="headerlink" href="#b612-logging-config-insecure-listen" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b612-test-for-insecure-use-of-logging-config-listen">
<h2>B612: Test for insecure use of logging.config.listen<a class="headerlink" href="#b612-test-for-insecure-use-of-logging-config-listen" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>This plugin test checks for the unsafe usage of the
<code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">logging.config.listen</span></code> function. The logging.config.listen
function provides the ability to listen for external
configuration files on a socket server. Because portions of the
configuration are passed through eval(), use of this function
may open its users to a security risk. While the function only
binds to a socket on localhost, and so does not accept connections
from remote machines, there are scenarios where untrusted code
could be run under the account of the process which calls listen().</p>
<p>logging.config.listen provides the ability to verify bytes received
across the socket with signature verification or encryption/decryption.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B612:logging_config_listen] Use of insecure
logging.config.listen detected.
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-94 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/94.html)
   Location: examples/logging_config_insecure_listen.py:3:4
2
3   t = logging.config.listen(9999)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.config.html#logging.config.listen">https://docs.python.org/3/library/logging.config.html#logging.config.listen</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 1.7.5.</span></p>
</div>
</section>
</section>

          <div role="main" class="document" itemscope="itemscope" itemtype="http://schema.org/Article">
           <div itemprop="articleBody">
             
  <section id="b613-trojansource">
<h1>B613: trojansource<a class="headerlink" href="#b613-trojansource" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b613-trojansource-bidirectional-control-characters">
<h2>B613: TrojanSource - Bidirectional control characters<a class="headerlink" href="#b613-trojansource-bidirectional-control-characters" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>This plugin checks for the presence of unicode bidirectional control characters
in Python source files. Those characters can be embedded in comments and strings
to reorder source code characters in a way that changes its logic.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B613:trojansource] A Python source file contains bidirectional control characters (&#39;\u202e&#39;).
   Severity: High   Confidence: Medium
   CWE: CWE-838 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/838.html)
   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/1.7.5/plugins/b113_trojansource.html
   Location: examples/trojansource.py:4:25
 3          access_level = &quot;user&quot;
 4      if access_level != &#39;none‮⁦&#39;: # Check if admin ⁩⁦&#39; and access_level != &#39;user
 5          print(&quot;You are an admin.\n&quot;)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://trojansource.codes/">https://trojansource.codes/</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cve.mitre.org/cgi-bin/cvename.cgi?name=CVE-2021-42574">https://cve.mitre.org/cgi-bin/cvename.cgi?name=CVE-2021-42574</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 1.7.10.</span></p>
</div>
</section>
</section>

  <section id="b614-pytorch-load">
<h1>B614: pytorch_load<a class="headerlink" href="#b614-pytorch-load" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b614-test-for-unsafe-pytorch-load">
<h2>B614: Test for unsafe PyTorch load<a class="headerlink" href="#b614-test-for-unsafe-pytorch-load" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>This plugin checks for unsafe use of <cite>torch.load</cite> and
<cite>torch.serialization.load</cite>. Using <cite>torch.load</cite> or
<cite>torch.serialization.load</cite> with untrusted data can lead to arbitrary
code execution. There are two safe alternatives:</p>
<ol class="arabic simple">
<li><p>Use <cite>torch.load</cite> with <cite>weights_only=True</cite> where only tensor data is
extracted, and no arbitrary Python objects are deserialized</p></li>
<li><p>Use the <cite>safetensors</cite> library from huggingface, which provides a safe
deserialization mechanism</p></li>
</ol>
<p>With <cite>weights_only=True</cite>, PyTorch enforces a strict type check, ensuring
that only torch.Tensor objects are loaded.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Use of unsafe PyTorch load
Severity: Medium   Confidence: High
CWE: CWE-502 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html)
Location: examples/pytorch_load_save.py:8
7    loaded_model.load_state_dict(torch.load(&#39;model_weights.pth&#39;))
8    another_model.load_state_dict(torch.load(&#39;model_weights.pth&#39;,
        map_location=&#39;cpu&#39;))
9
10   print(&quot;Model loaded successfully!&quot;)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/502.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://pytorch.org/docs/stable/generated/torch.load.html#torch.load">https://pytorch.org/docs/stable/generated/torch.load.html#torch.load</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://github.com/huggingface/safetensors">https://github.com/huggingface/safetensors</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 1.7.10.</span></p>
</div>

  <section id="b615-huggingface-unsafe-download">
<h1>B615: huggingface_unsafe_download<a class="headerlink" href="#b615-huggingface-unsafe-download" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b615-test-for-unsafe-hugging-face-hub-downloads">
<h2>B615: Test for unsafe Hugging Face Hub downloads<a class="headerlink" href="#b615-test-for-unsafe-hugging-face-hub-downloads" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>This plugin checks for unsafe downloads from Hugging Face Hub without proper
integrity verification. Downloading models, datasets, or files without
specifying a revision based on an immmutable revision (commit) can
lead to supply chain attacks where malicious actors could
replace model files and use an existing tag or branch name
to serve malicious content.</p>
<p>The secure approach is to:</p>
<ol class="arabic simple">
<li><p>Pin to specific revisions/commits when downloading models, files or datasets</p></li>
</ol>
<p>Common unsafe patterns:
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">AutoModel.from_pretrained(&quot;org/model-name&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">AutoModel.from_pretrained(&quot;org/model-name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;main&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">AutoModel.from_pretrained(&quot;org/model-name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;v1.0.0&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">load_dataset(&quot;org/dataset-name&quot;)</span></code> without revision
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">load_dataset(&quot;org/dataset-name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;main&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">load_dataset(&quot;org/dataset-name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;v1.0&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(&quot;org/model-name&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(&quot;org/model-name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;main&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(&quot;org/model-name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;v3.3.0&quot;)</span></code>
- <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">hf_hub_download(repo_id=&quot;org/model_name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">filename=&quot;file_name&quot;)</span></code>
- <a href="#id1"><span class="problematic" id="id2">``</span></a>hf_hub_download(repo_id=”org/model_name”,</p>
<blockquote>
<div><p>filename=”file_name”,
revision=”main”
)``</p>
</div></blockquote>
<ul>
<li><dl>
<dt><a href="#id3"><span class="problematic" id="id4">``</span></a>hf_hub_download(repo_id=”org/model_name”,</dt><dd><blockquote>
<div><p>filename=”file_name”,
revision=”v2.0.0”</p>
</div></blockquote>
<p>)``</p>
</dd>
</dl>
</li>
<li><p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">snapshot_download(repo_id=&quot;org/model_name&quot;)</span></code></p></li>
<li><p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">snapshot_download(repo_id=&quot;org/model_name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;main&quot;)</span></code></p></li>
<li><p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">snapshot_download(repo_id=&quot;org/model_name&quot;,</span> <span class="pre">revision=&quot;refs/pr/1&quot;)</span></code></p></li>
</ul>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Unsafe Hugging Face Hub download without revision pinning
Severity: Medium   Confidence: High
CWE: CWE-494 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/494.html)
Location: examples/huggingface_unsafe_download.py:8
7    # Unsafe: no revision specified
8    model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(&quot;org/model_name&quot;)
9
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/494.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/494.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://huggingface.co/docs/huggingface_hub/en/guides/download">https://huggingface.co/docs/huggingface_hub/en/guides/download</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 1.8.6.</span></p>
</div>
</section>

          <div role="main" class="document" itemscope="itemscope" itemtype="http://schema.org/Article">
           <div itemprop="articleBody">
             
  <section id="b701-jinja2-autoescape-false">
<h1>B701: jinja2_autoescape_false<a class="headerlink" href="#b701-jinja2-autoescape-false" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b701-test-for-not-auto-escaping-in-jinja2">
<h2>B701: Test for not auto escaping in jinja2<a class="headerlink" href="#b701-test-for-not-auto-escaping-in-jinja2" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>Jinja2 is a Python HTML templating system. It is typically used to build web
applications, though appears in other places well, notably the Ansible
automation system. When configuring the Jinja2 environment, the option to use
autoescaping on input can be specified. When autoescaping is enabled, Jinja2
will filter input strings to escape any HTML content submitted via template
variables. Without escaping HTML input the application becomes vulnerable to
Cross Site Scripting (XSS) attacks.</p>
<p>Unfortunately, autoescaping is False by default. Thus this plugin test will
warn on omission of an autoescape setting, as well as an explicit setting of
false. A HIGH severity warning is generated in either of these scenarios.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Using jinja2 templates with autoescape=False is dangerous and can
lead to XSS. Use autoescape=True to mitigate XSS vulnerabilities.
   Severity: High   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-94 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/94.html)
   Location: ./examples/jinja2_templating.py:11
10  templateEnv = jinja2.Environment(autoescape=False,
    loader=templateLoader)
11  Environment(loader=templateLoader,
12              load=templateLoader,
13              autoescape=False)
14

&gt;&gt; Issue: By default, jinja2 sets autoescape to False. Consider using
autoescape=True or use the select_autoescape function to mitigate XSS
vulnerabilities.
   Severity: High   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-94 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/94.html)
   Location: ./examples/jinja2_templating.py:15
14
15  Environment(loader=templateLoader,
16              load=templateLoader)
17
18  Environment(autoescape=select_autoescape([&#39;html&#39;, &#39;htm&#39;, &#39;xml&#39;]),
19              loader=templateLoader)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://www.owasp.org/index.php/Cross-site_Scripting_(XSS)">OWASP XSS</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://realpython.com/primer-on-jinja-templating/">https://realpython.com/primer-on-jinja-templating/</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://jinja.palletsprojects.com/en/2.11.x/api/#autoescaping">https://jinja.palletsprojects.com/en/2.11.x/api/#autoescaping</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_cross-site-scripting-xss.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_cross-site-scripting-xss.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/94.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/94.html</a></p></li>

  <section id="b702-use-of-mako-templates">
<h1>B702: use_of_mako_templates<a class="headerlink" href="#b702-use-of-mako-templates" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b702-test-for-use-of-mako-templates">
<h2>B702: Test for use of mako templates<a class="headerlink" href="#b702-test-for-use-of-mako-templates" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p>Mako is a Python templating system often used to build web applications. It is
the default templating system used in Pylons and Pyramid. Unlike Jinja2 (an
alternative templating system), Mako has no environment wide variable escaping
mechanism. Because of this, all input variables must be carefully escaped
before use to prevent possible vulnerabilities to Cross Site Scripting (XSS)
attacks.</p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: Mako templates allow HTML/JS rendering by default and are
inherently open to XSS attacks. Ensure variables in all templates are
properly sanitized via the &#39;n&#39;, &#39;h&#39; or &#39;x&#39; flags (depending on context).
For example, to HTML escape the variable &#39;data&#39; do ${ data |h }.
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-80 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/80.html)
   Location: ./examples/mako_templating.py:10
9
10  mako.template.Template(&quot;hern&quot;)
11  template.Template(&quot;hern&quot;)
</pre></div>
</div>
<div class="admonition seealso">
<p class="admonition-title">See also</p>
<ul class="simple">
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://www.makotemplates.org/">https://www.makotemplates.org/</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://owasp.org/www-community/attacks/xss/">OWASP XSS</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_cross-site-scripting-xss.html">https://security.openstack.org/guidelines/dg_cross-site-scripting-xss.html</a></p></li>
<li><p><a class="reference external" href="https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/80.html">https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/80.html</a></p></li>
</ul>
</div>
<div class="versionadded">
<p><span class="versionmodified added">Added in version 0.10.0.</span></p>
</div>
<div class="versionchanged">
<p><span class="versionmodified changed">Changed in version 1.7.3: </span>CWE information added</p>
</div>

  <section id="b703-django-mark-safe">
<h1>B703: django_mark_safe<a class="headerlink" href="#b703-django-mark-safe" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<dl class="py function">
<dt class="sig sig-object py">
<span class="sig-prename descclassname"><span class="pre">bandit.plugins.django_xss.</span></span><span class="sig-name descname"><span class="pre">django_mark_safe</span></span><span class="sig-paren">(</span><em class="sig-param"><span class="n"><span class="pre">context</span></span></em><span class="sig-paren">)</span><a class="reference internal" href="../_modules/bandit/plugins/django_xss.html#django_mark_safe"><span class="viewcode-link"><span class="pre">[source]</span></span></a></dt>
<dd><p><strong>B703: Potential XSS on mark_safe function</strong></p>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B703:django_mark_safe] Potential XSS on mark_safe function.
   Severity: Medium Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-80 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/80.html)
   Location: examples/mark_safe_insecure.py:159:4
   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/latest/plugins/b703_django_mark_safe.html
158         str_arg = &#39;could be insecure&#39;
159     safestring.mark_safe(str_arg)
</pre></div>

  <section id="b704-markupsafe-markup-xss">
<h1>B704: markupsafe_markup_xss<a class="headerlink" href="#b704-markupsafe-markup-xss" title="Link to this heading"></a></h1>
<section id="b704-potential-xss-on-markupsafe-markup-use">
<h2>B704: Potential XSS on markupsafe.Markup use<a class="headerlink" href="#b704-potential-xss-on-markupsafe-markup-use" title="Link to this heading"></a></h2>
<p><code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">markupsafe.Markup</span></code> does not perform any escaping, so passing dynamic
content, like f-strings, variables or interpolated strings will potentially
lead to XSS vulnerabilities, especially if that data was submitted by users.</p>
<p>Instead you should interpolate the resulting <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">markupsafe.Markup</span></code> object,
which will perform escaping, or use <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">markupsafe.escape</span></code>.</p>
<p><strong>Config Options:</strong></p>
<p>This plugin allows you to specify additional callable that should be treated
like <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">markupsafe.Markup</span></code>. By default we recognize <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">flask.Markup</span></code> as
an alias, but there are other subclasses or similar classes in the wild
that you may wish to treat the same.</p>
<p>Additionally there is a whitelist for callable names, whose result may
be safely passed into <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">markupsafe.Markup</span></code>. This is useful for escape
functions like e.g. <code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">bleach.clean</span></code> which don’t themselves return
<code class="docutils literal notranslate"><span class="pre">markupsafe.Markup</span></code>, so they need to be wrapped. Take care when using
this setting, since incorrect use may introduce false negatives.</p>
<p>These two options can be set in a shared configuration section
<cite>markupsafe_xss</cite>.</p>
<div class="highlight-yaml notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span><span class="nt">markupsafe_xss</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Recognize additional aliases</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">extend_markup_names</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">webhelpers.html.literal</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">my_package.Markup</span>

<span class="w">    </span><span class="c1"># Allow the output of these functions to pass into Markup</span>
<span class="w">    </span><span class="nt">allowed_calls</span><span class="p">:</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">bleach.clean</span>
<span class="w">        </span><span class="p p-Indicator">-</span><span class="w"> </span><span class="l l-Scalar l-Scalar-Plain">my_package.sanitize</span>
</pre></div>
</div>
<dl class="field-list simple">
<dt class="field-odd">Example<span class="colon">:</span></dt>
<dd class="field-odd"><p></p></dd>
</dl>
<div class="highlight-none notranslate"><div class="highlight"><pre><span></span>&gt;&gt; Issue: [B704:markupsafe_markup_xss] Potential XSS with
   ``markupsafe.Markup`` detected. Do not use ``Markup``
   on untrusted data.
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: High
   CWE: CWE-79 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/79.html)
   Location: ./examples/markupsafe_markup_xss.py:5:0
4       content = &quot;&lt;script&gt;alert(&#39;Hello, world!&#39;)&lt;/script&gt;&quot;
5       Markup(f&quot;unsafe {content}&quot;)
6       flask.Markup(&quot;unsafe {}&quot;.format(content))
</pre></div>
</div>
